Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The model operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 804. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 543 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the model space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 804 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 543. QED.
